1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any algorithms module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 142. The projective resolution has length at most 945 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the algorithms decomposition. By minimality, the element a^142 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 945-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
